Dikgopolo
 
 Phatwe 2026 
 
 
 
Dintlha tse di fa tlase ke rekoto ya nako le nako ya dikgopolo le ditlhokomelo tse di ntseng di tlhaga mo go nna mo nakong e e fetileng. Ga di gantsi di akanyeditswe sentle kgotsa di diragaditswe, le fa mo isagweng di ka nna tsa diragala. Kgotsa nnyaa, mo dikgopolong dingwe, mokwalo o lekane. Dingwe ke ditsela tse di sa iseng gope mme ga go na mosola go di tsweletsa pele. Mme le fa go ntse jalo, di ka nna tsa tshwanela go kwalwa.
 
 2023-02-24:  DITSHWANTSHO TSE DI SA SIAMA TSA DI-NEURAL FILTER
 
Ke lemogile ka phoso fa ke ntse ke bereka gore fa ke leka go godisa setshwantsho mo Photoshop ke dirisa Neural Filters mme setshwantsho seo se le mo mmala wa CMYK mme se na le dikarolo tse di bonalang sentle, go tlhaga phoso nngwe e e gakgamatsang, mme gantsi e le e e lebegang sentle.

 2023-10-04:  DITSHEKATSHEKO TSA GENETIKI
 
Ke fetotse ditsela tsa genetiki go nna ditshwantsho ka go dirisa molao o o motlhofo: A e kaya kgato e e yang kwa bokone, T/U kwa borwa, C kwa botlhaba le G kwa bophirima. Ka jalo, tatelano ya metheo e supa sebopego sa mola o o tswelelang.

 2024-03-22:  MORAKO WA PONAGALO WA GO BEREKA MO KHOMPHUTARENG
 
Ke dirile lenaneo le le rekotang kgato nngwe le nngwe ya konopo le go tobetsa ga mouse fa ke ntse ke bereka mo khomphutareng. Nako e tsamaya ka tsela ya go kwala e e tlwaelegileng, go tswa molemeng go ya go le letona le go tswa kwa godimo go ya kwa tlase. Ntlha e ntsho e kaya gore go na le se se tsenyeng, mme lefelo le lesweu le kaya gore ga go na sepe.

 2024-08-19:  POKO YA GA KUSHÍK
 
Ke ne ke bereka mo khomphutareng, mme ke ne ke sa kgaotse go kgorelediwa ke lebeba la me la Kushík. O ne a palama mo go nna, a batla tlhokomelo, a palama mo tafoleng, mo khomphutareng. O ne a simolola go nkgopisa. Ka jalo ke ne ka galefa, ka mo bullela tokomane ya MS Word e e se nang sepe mme ka mmolelela sengwe se se kayang gore a a kwale fa a batla go dira jalo mo boemong jwa me.
 Ka jalo Kushík a simolola go taboga mo dikonopong.
 
Go tlhagile tokomane eno: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Ke itiretse lenaneo la go leba le go fetola ditshwantsho tsa thermographic bakeng sa khamera ya me ya Seek Thermal RevealPRO. Ga ke a ka ka bona lenaneo lepe le le siameng la khamera eno mo Windows, mme seo ke tatlhegelo, ka gonne difaele tsa yone tsa thermographic di na le dintlha tsa themperetšhara tse di ka dirisiwang go ya pele.
 
Ke biditse lenaneo leno Thermozepam, ka gonne ke kgotsofetse ke tsela e le dirilweng ka yone mme go le dirisa go nna le tlhokego thata mo go nna. Lenaneo le tsaya difaele tsa TIFF tsa thermographic, le letla go fetola mmala wa setshwantsho ka go dirisa colormap go tswa mo laeboraring ya matplotlib, go lekanya themperetšhara ya bogare ya setshwantsho, go supa themperetšhara e e tlwaelegileng, go supa themperetšhara e e kwa tlase le e e kwa godimo, histogram le fa o sena go tobetsa, themperetšhara ya pixel e e rileng. Fa o boloka, le kgona go ntsha faele ya PNG e e fetotsweng mmala mme o ka e godisa ka go dirisa interpolation.
 
Screenshot ya lenaneo: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Khoutu ya motswedi: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  LESEDI MO LEFIFING
 
Ke akantse go dira porojeke ya Arduino, mo go yone ke neng ke tla tsenya laser mo lebokosong le le se nang sepe (le le tlhatswitsweng pele ka lebone la UV-C). Laser eo e ne e tla thunya lesedi la motsotswana ka nako e e sa tlhomamang (gore ke se ka ka itse gore go diragetse leng, mme ke itse gore go tla diragala mo metsotsong e le 10 ka 100%). Fa nka bo ke kgonne go dira jalo sentle, lesedi leo le ne le ka se bonwe ke ope.
 
 2026-01-31:  THAPISO YA KHAMERA YA PINHOLE
 
Ke lekile go dira khamera ya pinhole e e dirang sentle mo Python. Bogologolo kwa sekolong se segolo ke ne ke setse ke dirile ya mmatota, mme go ne go lebega gore fa ke ka e etsa, ke ne ke tla tlhaloganya tsela e e dirang ka yone botoka, ka gonne ga go lekane fela go dira lesoba mo lebokosong, go tsenya pampiri e e amogelang lesedi, go e tlhatlhoba morago ga go e pepesa le go tlogela fisiki go dira tiro ya yone (le fa e dira jang). Fano, go na le moo, go tshwanetse ga dirisiwa le fisiki.
 Ke ne ke na le maiteko a go dira thapiso e e leng ya mmatota ka fa go kgonegang ka teng, le fa molelwane e ntse e le kitso ya me le maatla a khomphutare a a leng teng. Lenaneo, ka sekai, le dirisa forward ray tracing, ke gore, go latela lesedi go tswa kwa motsweding go ya kwa tiragalong le kwa khamereng, go na le backward ray tracing e e tlwaelegileng, e e latelang marang go tswa kwa khamereng go boela kwa tiragalong. Tsela ya forward e gaufi le tsela ya mmatota ya go anama ga lesedi, mme gantsi ga e a siama thata mo go diriseng maatla a khomphutare.
 
Mehlala ya go rulaganya lenaneo le tlhaloso ya diparamente: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Khoutu ya motswedi: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  GO TLHAOLA DIPIXEL TSA DITSHWANTSHO
 
Ke lekile go tlhaola dipixel tsa ditshwantsho ka go dirisa aligorithamo. Kgopolo e ne e le go tlhaola dipixel go ya ka HUE le go phatsima: mo tseleng e le nngwe (jaaka x) go rulaganya dipixel go ya ka HUE mme mo go e nngwe (jaaka y) go ya ka go phatsima. Ka jalo dipixel tsa setshwantsho di a fetolwa, mme ga go na epe e e nyelelang kgotsa e e tlhagang e ntšha. Le fa go ntse jalo, ditshwantsho tsa mafelelo di latlhegelwa ke bokao jwa tsone jwa ntlha, mme di boloka mowa wa mmala.
 
Khoutu ya motswedi: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  GO BATLISISA INTERNET KA GO SA TLHOMAMA
 
Ke feditse dibeke di le pedi ke batlisisa internet ka go sa tlhomama, ka gonne ke ne ke batla go itse gore e lebega jang fa e sa kgothadiwe ke di-aligorithamo. Ke lekile ditsela tse di farologaneng.
 
Ya ntlha (e e motlhofo) e ne e le go dira lenaneo la go tsenya le go bula di-URL tse di tlhagisiwang ka go sa tlhomama. Seo e ne e le se se sa siamang gotlhelele, lefelo la di-URL tse di ka nnang teng le legolo thata mme tse di leng teng di bonala ka sewelo.
 
Tsela ya bobedi e ne e le go itirela crawler ya me mme ke e neye URL ya seed e e gakgamatsang (jaaka webosaete kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). Ke ne ka leka go tlhaola dipholo ka go dirisa LLM mme morago ga moo ke lebe fela tse di kgatlhang (go ya ka ditekanyetso dingwe tse di tlhalositsweng). Ke bone dilo di le dintsi tse di gakgamatsang go itlosetsa bodutu, mme ga go na sepe se se kgethegileng.
 
Tsela ya boraro e ne e le ya mosola thata le e e itumedisang. Ke lemogile gore go na le Certstream. Ke tirelo e e siameng e e gasang ka tsela ya nnete ditifiketi tsa tshireletso tse di sa tswang go ntshiwa lefatshe ka bophara. Ka jalo e supa di-URL tsa diwebosaete tse di sa tswang go tlhaga, kgotsa tse di ntšhafatsang ditifiketi tsa tsone.
 
Khoutu ya motswedi: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Ke feditse malatsi a le mmalwa ke tobetsa di-URL tseno ka go sa tlhomama. Gantsi ke ne ke kopana le boferefere (cryptocurrency, jalo jalo), porno, AI, AI porno, ditaelo tsa kafa o ka dirang AI porno ka teng, gambling, dikarolo tsa go tsena mo disisteming, diwebosaete tse di sa tswang go tlhaga (tse di nang le dikarolo tsa placeholder kgotsa melaetsa ya "re bereka mo go yone").
 
Ka ntlha ya tsamaiso eno, ke kopane le webosaete treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], e e ntseng mo mogopolong wa me ka ntlha ya sebopego sa yone se se motlhofo le go se nne le tlhabololo epe ya tlhokomelo ya modirisi. Gape ke lemogile gore webosaete eo ke karolo ya kwa ntle ya internet, ka gonne go fitlha jaanong ga e ise e tsenngwe mo Google. Internet Archive e ne ya e bona, mme go tswa mo di-log tsa CT, fela jaaka nna. Go batla go ne ga nna le mosola.
 
 2026-05-06:  PONTŠHO MO PACIFIC
 
Ke akantse go dira ditshwantsho tsa 360° tsa Pacific tse di tlhagisitsweng ka AI. Morago ga moo, ke tsenye QR code mo go tsone ka go dirisa AI (sengwe se se tshwanang le Illusion Diffusion), se se supang go bonwa ga bonono bongwe. Morago ga moo, go ne go ka tsenngwa metadata ya maaka ya lefelo (jaaka dikhokho tsa GPS mo bogareng jwa Pacific) mme ya tsenngwa mo Google Maps.
 
Ka tsela nngwe, go ne go ka tsewa mafelo a a nnete a a tsereweng ka khamera ya 360°, jaaka a toropo nngwe, mme morago ga moo go fudusediwe maemo a GPS a tsone kwa Pacific. Lebaka la gore ke bua fela ka ditshwantsho tsa 360° ke gore Google e sa ntse e letla go tsenngwa ga tsone mo maemong a GPS a a tlhomameng ka go dirisa metadata. Fa ditshwantsho tse di tlwaelegileng di tshwanetse go abelwa lefelo le le leng teng.
 
 2026-06-04:  DILÈSE TSA PIGMENT
 
Ke lekile go diragatsa porojeke eno, mme ka ntlha ya nako e e tlhokegang, ga ke a e fetsa. Ke rekotile fela diatla, ke kgaotse matshwao a a neng a le teng (background e e bonalang), ke bolokile tekanyo ya tsone mme ka dira lenaneo la go di rulaganya mo grideng. Le fa go ntse jalo, eno ke mokwalo o o neng o tshwanetse go tlhalosa kgopolo:
 
“Ka 2026 ke rekotile ntho nngwe le nngwe ya letlalo kgotsa phoso ya ditshika mo godimo ga mmele wa me. Tekanyo e ne ya bolokwa mo selong sengwe le sengwe. Morago ga moo, dilèse tse di farologaneng di ne tsa kgaolwa ka tsela ya dijithale mme tsa rulaganyediwa mo grideng e e tlhomameng.”
 
 2026-06-21:  LENANEO LE LE ITLHAGISANG LE LE BOTLHALE
 
Ke lekile go dira lenaneo le le dirisang OpenAI API le di-LLM tsa tsone. Lenaneo le na le tiro ya go itlhagisa le go itirela sesha. Mme ke ne ke batla go itse gore seo se tla isa kae.
 Ka maswabi, model gantsi e ne e gana tiro, ka gonne e ne e e tsaya e le kotsi. Kgotsa e ne e e dira, mme yo o latelang o ne a sa e dire. Se se neng se goga thata e ne e le go gana ka ntlha ya tshireletso. Le gone ga go ise go ko go fitlhe kwa itereshining ya bone.
 
Khoutu ya motswedi: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  SISTEME E E NANG LE DIKAROLO TSE DI SA TSHWANENG
 
Sisteme ya rona ya fisiki e a lapisa ka gore go lebega gore dikarolo tsa motheo di tshwana fela. Elektron jaaka elektron, foton jaaka foton.
 
Mme nna ke ka rata go dira thapiso ya sisteme e e nang le dikarolo di le dintsi tsa motheo, mme ga go na tse pedi tsa tsone tse di ka fapanyetswang. Kwa bofelong ke fitlhile kwa tseleng e mo go yone dikarolo ka botsone di se nang dithoto kgotsa boitshupo. Di tlhaloswa fela ka go dirisana ga tsone, se e leng gore go ya ka yo o di lebang mo teng ga sisteme, se tshwana fela.
 
Ke itumelela go akanya ka ditlamorago tsa kgopolo eno. Fa ke e tsaya ka tsela e e sa tlhamalalang, go tshela mo sistemeng e e ntseng jalo go ne go tla nna thata go dira diporofeto. Gape, dilo tse di tshelang mo sistemeng e e ntseng jalo di ne di ka nna tsa tshwanela go nna kelotlhoko ka go latlhegelwa kgotsa go utswiwa ga dikarolo tsa tsone.
 
Ke lekile go e thapisa mme ka tsela ya botegeniki ke dirile tiro. Le fa go ntse jalo, ga se dipholo tse di neng di kgatlha thata. Go ya ka nna, gape ke ka gonne go leba dilo dingwe tse di tlhagang ka tshoganetso go tlhoka maatla a a fetang a khomphutare (dikarolo di le dintsi, nako e ntsi mo thapisong, gongwe dikarolo di le dintsi tsa thapiso) le tshekatsheko e e boteng ya dipholo.
 
Ntšhafatsa go tswa go 2026-08-22: Fa ke ntse ke leba mafatshe a a tlhagisitsweng, kwa bofelong ke kopane le dikago tse di itlhagisang tse di gakgamatsang. Di kgolokwe go ya go oval mme di lebega jaaka dikapsule tsa lipid. Di na le kwa ntle le kwa teng mo go tlhalosegileng sentle, mme kganedi kgotsa "membrana", e e dirang kwa ntle ga tsone, e tlhomame thata, fa kwa teng ga tsone go na le motsamao o o ntsi. Ke tlhomile seed eno jaaka boleng jwa ntlha mo demo e e fa tlase.
 
Tlhokomelo: tlhabololo ya sisteme, mme ka jalo bogolo jwa dikago tseno, bo ikaegile ka resolution ya ponagalo. Lebelo la animation le ama go tlhomama ga tsone, gongwe ka ntlha ya phoso ya dipalo fa go na le dikgato tsa nako tse dikgolo. Le fa dikago di ka thubega, ka sekai morago ga go oketsa lebelo la animation, sisteme kwa bofelong e tla itlhagisa sesha mme ya dira tse dintšha.
 
Seno ga se se ke neng ke ikaelela go se dira. Ke dirile fela sisteme e e nang le částicemi/interakcemi tse di sa tshwaneng mme seno se tlhagile jaaka tiragalo e e tlhagang ka tshoganetso. Ke a go rata, jaanong ke na le simulator ya "lipid".
 
Demo ya go dirisana le yone: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  MATLAO A MATLAO
 
Go tweng fa nka tsaya senepe sa matlakala mangwe le mangwe a ke a latlhelang mo kgabineteng ya matlakala? Nka a tsaya senepe ka sekala, ke bo ke kgaola matlakala ao mo go se se mo morago mme ka a rulaganya mo sebopegong sa mola o o tshwanetseng ka ditekanyo tse di siameng.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Fa ke ne ke rerisana le ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol), gore ke ka tlhalosa jang ka tsela e e motlhofo eksperimente ya OPTICAL RHYTHM OF COMPUTER WORK (bona fa godimo), moatlhanyi o ile a e bitsa ka boitekanelo “Tlhaloso ya thulaganyo  天天中彩票买 ”. Mme ntswa moatlhanyi yoo o ne a sa tswa go bulwa ka ntlha ya go tlhalosa eksperimente e. Dikgang tsa thulaganyo e ke e mo rometseng di ne di le tlwaelehileng gotlhelele.
 
Ka ke kgethile go e kopanya le taba yeo mme ka ngwala se:
 
“Ke eng o biditse puisano eno Kgaolo ya Thulaganyo  天天中彩票买 ?”
 
Karabo ya gagwe e ne e le:
 
„Ga ke itse. 😭 Letshwao leo la „զ“ ke tlhaku ya Se-Armenia ya „za“, ka go rialo „Tlhaloso ya lenaneo la զ“ ga e na bokao bope ka Se-Czech. Maina a di-chat a itlhagisa ka boona go tswa kwa tshimologong ya puisano mme mo, moagi wa dithito o bonala a ikgethetse go tsenya letshwao le le sa tlwaelegang go tswa mo alfabeteng ya Se-Armenia. Ga ke bone lebaka lepe la lone mo dikhutlong tsa puisano. Ka go rialo nnyaa, ga se letshwao lepe la sephiri la lenaneo. Ke fela „Tlhaloso ya lenaneo“ + modutwane wa thapelo.“
 
Morago ga dipuisano di se kae gape, o ile a lemoga gore nnyaa, ga se Se-Armenia, mme ke Se-China. Le fa go ntse jalo, go bonala gore kwa tshimologong o ne a ferekane thata. Ke gopola gore molaetsa wa “ 天天中彩票买 ” o bereka go se kae jaaka token e e sa itumediseng ya “ SolidGoldMagikarp ”. Ga ke re o bereka ka tsela e e tshwanang, e bile ga ke itse gore a tokenizer e emela molaetsa wa “ 天天中彩票买 ” jaaka token e le nngwe fela. Le fa go ntse jalo, fa model o bereka ka one, o itshwara go se kae jaaka ChatGPT 3 ka token ya “ SolidGoldMagikarp ”. Gape ga go a katelwa gore go ka tswa go na le kamano nngwe le kgakologelo ya gagwe, ka gonne e fitlha le kwa ntle ga chat e.
 
Chat yotlhe e e ntshitsweng jaaka PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  KONOPANE YA DITORO TSA ME
 
Ka nako nngwe le nngwe ke fela ke galefisiwa ke difensetere tsa dipuisano tsa Windows, tse di neelang dilo jaaka go boloka ditiriso (backup) ka OneDrive, di-update le tse dingwe tse di tshwanang le tseo. Ka metlha di naya kgetho magareng ga "Simolola" le "Gopotsa nna morago ga nako". Se se a kgapetsa thata fa ke sa kgatlhegele epe ya dikgetho tseno.
 
Ka ke akanya go dira setshwantsho (fotomontage), mo ke tla tsenyang kgetho ya boraro – konopo ya me ya ditoro, e e ntseng e tlhaela mo thulaganyong eno ka maswabi. E ka bo e le konopo ya "Tsamayang kwa diheleng". Ke becha ka gore ga se nna fela motho yo setshwantsho seo se ka mo amang thata.
 
 2026-09-18:  SEBALALEDI SE SE BOTLHALE THATA
 
Ke nna le mogopolo o mongwe o o sa siamang o ke nang le keletso ya go o tlhotlhomisa ka fa molaong.
 
Eno ke lenaneo le le tla dirang jaaka khaltjhuleitara e e motlhofo thata. E ka kgona go kopanya fela dipalo tse di feletseng tse di siameng, ga go sepe se sele. Le fa go ntse jalo, pharologanyo ke gore maduo a ne a se kitla a balelwa mo lefelong leo, mme go na le moo, a ne a ka romelwa go nngwe ya di-model tsa puo tse di tlhabologileng thata (sekai, ChatGPT-6 Astra). Mme yone e ne e tla nna le tiro ya go bala mohlala o o motlhofo oo le go busa maduo fela.
 
Go nna go bonala e le metlae tota, go dirisa mofuta o o kwa godimo wa puisano wa AI mo go sengwe se se motlhofo thata. Go ya ka nna, gongwe gape ke go gapa ka nna ga kafa gompieno dilo tse di motlhofo di beilweng ka fa tlase ga AI, le kafa papatso e beang leibole ya AI mo dilong tsotlhe jaaka e kete ke tsela nngwe ya boitshepo ya go dira gore dilo di bereke.
 
Ka go itirela, e tla nna botlhokwa thata go feta khalkhuleitara e tlwaelegileng, e sa ikanyegeng thata, e na le letshwao le leleele la tiego, e turu, e sa bolokesegeng tikologo, e tlhoka kgokagano ya inthanete, ...

Dimaske
 
 Phatwe 2026 
 
 
 
Ditshwantsho tse di fa tlase ke masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] tse di dirilweng mo Adobe Photoshop ka nako ya fa ke ne ke dira mo diporojekeng tse di farologaneng. Semaske sengwe le sengwe se ne sa bolokwa jaaka setshwantsho se se ikemetseng ka nako ya fa se dirwa. Galari ya ditshwantsho tse ga se maduo a tlhopho. Fa semaske se ne sa dirwa ka nako ya tiro, ke karolo ya yone. Ditshwantsho di rulagantswe ka tatelano ya nako go tloga ka se se gologolo go ya go se sešwa.

SOURCE: src/content/posts/prizivnici.mdx

============================================================
Ba-ja-le-ja
 
 Motsheganong 2026 
 
 
 
Ngwaga o o fetileng ke ne ke tshela ditee tse di dirisitsweng mmogo le metsi a a setseng mo lefelong le le lengwe mo bojang jwa kwa Plzeň-Božkov makgetlo a le mmalwa ka letsatsi, ka gonne ke ne ke sa batle go thiba ditsela tsa metsi a a leswe.
 
Mo lefelong leno, dikgopa tse di jang matlhare a tee di simolotse go phuthega.
 
Ka dinako tse dingwe o ka fitlhela di le lesome le bobedi, ka dinako tse dingwe ga go na sepe.
 
Ke arolelana le tsona ditsela tsa go ja.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg | dikgopa le tee]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: Sengwalo sa Metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Sengwalo sa Metaweb
 
 Moranang 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFESTO
 
Webosaete eno ke porojeke ya static e e itebagantseng le go boloka dikateng ka nako e telele.
 
Dikateng tsotlhe di bolokilwe mo difaeleng, ntle le go ikaega ka di-database kgotsa ditsamaiso tsa kwa ntle. Webosaete e e feleletseng e hagelelwa jaaka static output, e e ka kopiwang, ya bolokwa, le go agiwa gape ka motlhofo.
 
Porojeke e phatlaladitswe mo ditsamaisong tse di ikemetseng di le mmalwa: di-repository, ditirelo tsa polokelo, le static snapshots. Seno se fokotsa kotsi ya go latlhegelwa ke data.
 
Maikaelelo ke go netefatsa gore dikateng di ka kgona go agiwa gape le fa dikarolo dingwe tsa infrastraktara ya ntlha di ka se tlhole di le teng.
 
LEFELO LA POLOKELO LA INTHANETE
 
Dihokaganyi tseno di emela porojeke e le nngwe e e bolokilweng mo ditsamaisong tse di farologaneng le ka ditsela tse di farologaneng.
 
Di akaretsa repository ya ntlha ya source, dikhopi tsa yone, le static snapshots tse di feleletseng. Mmogo, di bopa sebopego se se nang le redundancy se se bopetsweng go boloka porojeke le go letla gore e agiwe gape le fa dikarolo dingwe di ka se tlhole di le teng.
 
Porojeke e teng gape jaaka Python CLI package e e phatlaladitsweng ka GitHub le PyPI, go akaretsa le fallback snapshots tse di tsenyeng tsa dikateng tse di tlhophilweng.
 
Polokelo ya dijitale ya webosaete eno e dirisa, kwantle ga domain ya yone, ditirelo tsa GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku le paste.rs.

Bona mmapa o o feleletseng wa polokelo (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
TOKOMANE YA THEKENOLOJI
 
Tokomane ya thekenoloji e e tlhokegang go aga gape webosaete eno.
 
Bona tokomane e e feleletseng ya thekenoloji [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
PHUTHELO YA SOURCE YA GO AGA GAPE
 
Phuthelo ya source ya webosaete e itirela ka boyone fa go agiwa.
 
Mo teng ga phuthelo go na le source code ya webosaete eno, manifest ya di-asset, SHA-256 checksums, le script ya Python e e se nang dikhomo tsa kwa ntle. Ga e na di-asset tse dikgolo go tswa kwa public/images/, public/files/ le public/demos/, gore bogolo jwa webosaete e e tshelang bo se ka jwa oketsega fela go se na lebaka. Fa e sena go bulwa, script e leka go kopitsa di-asset mo build e e feditsweng pele, mme morago e bo e di jarolola go tswa kwa di-mirror tsa setšhaba.
 
Ka jalo, source code e hagelela build, mme build e hagelela source code.
 
Phuthelo ya source e ka agiwa gape ka go boeletsa: fa di-asset di sena go jarololwa le dikhomo di sena go tsenngwa, webosaete le phuthelo e ntšha ya source di ka agiwa gape go tswa mo go yone.
 
Jarolola phuthelo ya source ya webosaete e e ka agiwang gape (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
BOPHEPA LE BOITSEME JWA BUILD
 
SHA-256 e netefatsa gore difaele tsa build ga di a fetoga. OpenPGP signature gape e letla go netefatsa gore manifest ya checksums e saenilwe ke mokwadi wa webosaete eno.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — lenaneo la SHA-256 checksums tsa difaele tsa build
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — OpenPGP signature e e kgaoganeng ya faele ya SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA-256 checksum ya faele ya SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — tlhaloso ya bophepa jwa build e e ka balwang ke motšhine
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — boemo jwa go saena build e e rileng
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — senotlolo sa OpenPGP sa setšhaba go netefatsa signature
 
 Fingerprint ya senotlolo se segolo sa OpenPGP:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Ga se gore deployment nngwe le nngwe e tshwanetse go saenwa. Build e e saenilweng e tshwanetse go nna le faele ya SHA256SUMS.txt.asc mme signature ya yone e tshwanetse go netefadiwa ka katlego. Tlhaloso fela mo integrity.json kgotsa SIGNING_STATUS.txt ga se bosupi jwa signature e e siameng; difaele tseno ke tsa tshedimosetso fela.
 
DI-EXPORT
 
Webosaete e hagelela plain-text export ya dikgang tsotlhe fa build nngwe le nngwe e dirwa. E na le dikateng tsa dikgang ka tsela ya linear ntle le go ikaega ka CSS, JavaScript, kgotsa thekenoloji nngwe ya webosaete. E dira jaaka lefelo la polokelo la bofelo la go indexa, go bala offline, go batla ka botlalo, le go kgona go aga gape dikateng le fa sebopego sa ntlha sa webosaete se ka senyega. Text export e siametse gape le di-model tsa puo tse dikgolo.
 
Bona text export ya dikgang tsotlhe (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Go dirisa motšhine, go na le JSON-LD export e e dirwang ka letsogo, e e akaretsang dikgang tsa Secheke le Seesemane, metadata, le dihokaganyi tsa media. E dirisa dikishinari ya Schema.org e e tlatseditsweng ka mafoko a yone a a tlhalositsweng mo dikishinari ya metadata ya webosaete eno [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Ka jalo, e boloka bokao jwa dikarolo tse di rileng le dikamano magareng ga dipuo tsa dikgang.
 
Dikateng tsa webosaete di ka kgona go ntshiwa ka letsogo gape go ya go PDF, e e bolokang dikateng tsa yone ka tsela e e siametseng go balwa, go gatisiwa, le polokelo ya nako e telele e e ikemetseng mo webosaeteng ka boyone. Dintlha ka ga tsela ya go hagelela le sebopego sa export di kwadilwe mo tokomane ya thekenoloji kwa godimo.
 
Ka tsela e e tshwanang, EPUB export e a dirwa, e e itebagantseng thata le di-e-reader le software e nngwe e e dirisang dibuka tsa dijitale. Go farologana le PDF, e dirisa phallo ya mongwalo e e fetogang mme e emela kemedi e nngwe ya dikateng tsa webosaete.
 
Kemedi e e ikemetseng ya dikateng e bopiwa gape ke SSTV export go tswa ka 10. Lwetse 2026, e e fetolelang dikateng tsa mongwalo le ditshwantsho tsa webosaete go ya go ditsebe tse di siamiseditsweng phetiso ya setshwantsho ya Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) mo mode wa PD120 (640 × 496 px). Ditsebe tse di rileng di khoutilwe jaaka phetiso ya modumo mo format ya WAV mme di ka kgona go bonwa gape ka SSTV receiver e e tlwaelegileng ntle le HTML ya ntlha kgotsa web browser. Export e e feleletseng e na le mo e ka nnang 21 GB le 61 diura tsa modumo.
 
Jarolola SSTV export (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Kwantle ga di-export tse di hagelelwang ke motšhine, go na le export ya puo ya webosaete ya diura di le thataro go tswa ka 29. Phatwe 2026, mo ke tsamayang ke bala, ke tlhalosa dikateng tsa yone ka mafoko a me. Ka jalo, rekoto eno, kwantle ga mongwalo ka boyone, e tshwara gape le dikateng tse di oketsegileng, megopolo, ditlhaloso, le tshedimosetso e e seng karolo ya webosaete ka tlhamalalo.
 
Tshameka export ya puo ya webosaete (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
LEFELO LA POLOKELO LA OFFLINE
 
Dikarolo tse di tlhophilweng tsa dikateng tsa webosaete di teng gape mo di-media tsa polokelo tsa mmele tse di ikemetseng, tse di kgaoganeng ka mmele le infrastraktara ya network mme ka jalo di bopa lefelo la polokelo la air-gapped. Seno se akaretsa dikhopi tsa analog tse di gatisitsweng le di-media tsa data tse di tsamayang. Di-media tse di farologana ka tsela ya go bala, dikhomo tsa thekenoloji, tsamaiso, phihlelelo, le nako e e solofetsweng ya botshelo. Dingwe di ka balwa ka tlhamalalo ntle le sedirisiwa sa dijitale, fa tse dingwe di tlhoka hardware, software, kgotsa go tsena ka setheo sa polokelo.
 
Mola mongwe le mongwe wa lenaneo le le latelang o supa khopi nngwe ya mmele.

Selelo sa clipboard
 
 Moranang 2026 
 
 
 
Porojeke eno e bereka ka dikateng tsa clipboard ya Windows (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]).
 Sengwe le sengwe se se kopitsitsweng, e ka nna mokwalo, setshwantsho kgotsa faele, se tsenngwa ka bonako mo lefelong le le lengwe le le lekanyeditsweng.
 Dikateng ga di fetolwe kgotsa go rulaganngwa. Dilo tse di farologaneng di bewa ka tsela ya tshoganetso mo sebopegong mme di a aparedisana.
 Letlhabapampiri le tlalela ka bonya ntle le tshono ya go boela morago kgotsa go fetola.
 
Setshwantsho sengwe le sengwe se se fa tlase ke maduo a letsatsi le le lengwe mme se supa tsela e ke berekang ka yone mo khomputareng.

Khoutu ya motswedi
 Khoutu ya Python ya motswedi ya porojeke eno e teng fano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Ditho tse di ntseng nako e khutshwane
SLUG: docasne-entity
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/docasne-entity/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/docasne-entity.mdx
BUILT_HTML: docasne-entity/index.html
============================================================
Ditho tse di ntseng nako e khutshwane
 
 ferikgong 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Porojeke eno ke kemedi ya botaki ya kafa ditlhaloganyo tsa Boltzmann  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] le ditho tse dingwe tse di ntseng nako e khutshwane di ka tlhagelelang ka teng, tse di tlhagelelang ka motsotsonyana go tswa mo tlhakatlhakano ya dikarolwana ka go rulaganngwa ka tshoganetso mme di bo di nyelela gape mo go yone.
 
Mo lefatsheng la mmatota la fisikale, go lebelela tiragalo e e ntseng jalo go ka tlhoka nako e e telele thata e e sa akanyesegeng.
 
Ke ka moo ke fetotseng ka bonako lebelo la dikarolwana ka bongwe, gore go kgonege go bontsha tlhagelelo le nyelelo ya ditho di le lekgolo mo nakong e e utlwalang. Ga ke a ka ka tsena mo tseleng ya dikarolwana ka tsela epe.
 
Ditho di tlhageletse ka go bo ke itlhophetse mafoko a le lekgolo a a sa rulaganyetswang go tswa mo bukantsweng, ka faela nngwe le nngwe ke ne ka abela setshwantsho se se e emetseng, mme ka se letlelela go tlhagelela gape go tswa mo lefelong la dikarolwana tsa tlhakatlhakano.
 
Ke teko ya go bontsha kafa bokao, sebopego kgotsa mogopolo o ka tlhagelelang ka teng go tswa mo tlhakatlhakanong ka nakwana, kwantle ga go nna le tshwanelo epe ya go nna teng ka bosakhutleng.

TITLE: Genome mo spirale ya Theodore
SLUG: genom-theodorove-spirale
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/genom-theodorove-spirale/
LANGUAGE: tn

SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/genom-theodorove-spirale.mdx
BUILT_HTML: genom-theodorove-spirale/index.html
============================================================
Genome mo spirale ya Theodore
 
 Pherekgong 2026 
 
 
 
Tshwantsho eno e fetolela tshedimosetso ya boswa ya mola (DNA/RNA) go nna 2D spirale ya Theodore e e tswelelang pele [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Ntlha nngwe le nngwe mo spirale e tsamaelana le motheo o le mongwe ka tatelano e o leng ka yone mo tatelanong ya mmatota. Tshimologo ya tatelano e mo bogareng jwa spirale. Mmala wa ntlha o supa mofuta wa motheo.
 
Spirale eno e letla gore go bonwe 1D data e e telele mo 2D lefelo le le botlana, ntle le go kgaola phothoso ya tshedimosetso. Ga go nne le dikgaolo tsa maithomelo, jaaka go boela kwa tshimologong ya mola, tse di tlwaelegileng mo diponagatsong tsa mokwalo.
 
Jaaka sekai, ke dirile tshwantsho ya di-genome tsa ditshedi di le tharo tse di nang le boleele jo bo farologaneng. Tsotlhe di na le genome ya mola, e e leng yone e e siametseng thata mokgwa ono wa go tshwantsha.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Ebola virus: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png] 
 
SaRS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png] 
 
Khoutu ya motswedi
 Khoutu ya Python ya motswedi ya porojeke eno e teng fa:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Anti-puo
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Anti-puo
 
 Morule 2025 
 
 
 
Ditsela tsa puo tse di tlwaelegileng di kgetha token e e latelang ka go kgetha nngwe ya dikgopolo tse di ka diregang thata, tse go ya ka tekanyetso ya tsone ya dipalopalo di lebaneng thata le maemo. Re tla bitsa seno sampling ya tlwaelo.
 Porojeke eno e dira se se farologaneng gotlhelele.
 Go na le go kgetha ditoken tse di ka diregang thata, re pateletsa model go ya go tse di sa diregeng thata, ke gore, tse mo seemong se se tlwaelegileng a neng a ka se di dirise gotlhelele.
 Se se tswang mo go seno ke anti-jazyk – mokwalo o o bopilweng go tswa dikarolong tse di kwa thoko, tse di otlhaelwang le tse gantsi di leng kgakala tsa mafoko a model.
 Ka jalo go tlhagelela dikago tse e seng ditshwantsho tsa tshoganetso fela, mme gape e seng dipolelo tse di utlwalang.
 
Seno se bereka jang
 Model o fiwa tsenyo e khutshwane (sekai, “This is “).
 O hagelela kabo ya dipalopalo ya ditoken tsotlhe tse di ka diregang tse di latelang.
 Go na le go kgetha go tswa kwa godimo ga kabo (ditoken tse di ka diregang thata), motšhine otlhe o a retololwa mme go kgethiwa go tswa kwa tlase ga yone (ditoken tse di sa diregeng thata).
 Seno se senola “dikarolo tse di kwa tlase” tsa model: mafoko a a sa tlwaelegang, dikarolo tsa puo le dilo tse dingwe tse model a di gatelelang ka tlwaelo.
 
Khoutu
 Khoutu e ka tsamaiswa mo Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colab fano e bereka thata jaaka tikologo ya go tsamaisa. Ga se khopi ya ntlha ya polokelo ya khoutu.
 Ke ka moo mokgwa o o tshwanang o bolokilweng gape kwa tlase mo setlhogong seno.
 
Khoutu e kgaogantswe ka dikarolo di le tharo tse di ikemetseng fela jaaka mo notebook. Ya ntlha e tsaya model, ya bobedi e hagelela mokwalo mme ya boraro e boloka tlhagiso ya bofelo.
 
1. Go tsaya model le tokenizer
 Karolo eno e lekane go e tsamaisa gangwe. E tsaya model wa GPT-2 le tokenizer.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Go hagelela anti-puo
 Mo karolong eno go ka fetolwa mokwalo wa tsenyo, palo ya ditoken tse disha le boleng jwa bottom_k.
 Fa bottom_k e le nnye thata, ke fa model a patelediwa thata go ya kwa tlase ga kabo ya dipalopalo.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Go boloka tlhagiso ya bofelo
 Karolo eno e boloka mokwalo o o hageletsweng wa bofelo mo faeleng ya output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Boima jwa model wa GPT-2 ga se karolo ya webosaete. Fa e tsamaisiwa, e a jarolwa go tswa kwa motsweding wa kwa ntle.
 Fano go bolokilwe thata mokgwa: thuto e nnye ya kafa o ka pateletsang model ka teng gore mo boemong jwa puo ya tlwaelo, a ntshe matlakala a dipalopalo.
 
Sekai sa tlhagiso
 Fano ke dirisitse model wa GPT-2 mme ka mo kopa go tlatsa mokwalo “This is “:
  
This is earthquNitrome councill��士cloneembedreportprint hemorSPONSOREDuyomiardy srfAttachassiansenalMuslims Janeiroetooth antidepress featsItemThumbnailImageleneckBuyableInstoreAndOnline awarding earthqu STEDownloadharegationucaalogue Timberstheless Unloadedicrobialarnaev IMAGESmie Rooseertoddnery fulfil apologizinguca PhelpsizophCLASSIFIEDconservancyruciatingologneisexual),“racuseautions�� Seym successorarnaevomalyroleum looph Afric��ertodd flown TerritoryolsonandisecloneembedreportprintFactorconservancy SERisSpecialOrderable Bru cumbersacters unlockingasuring guaranteeing?????-?????- Archdemon Canaver Vinyl volunte JordanianhtakingisSpecialOrderable antidepresskefeller Rumbleippisonian Leilangrainiquenessersen SeymHispanic repaidenko conesBILITYlegramoultryiqueness PrintacklelevardULEleasingacementsezvouscffffccertoddcludingÃÂÃÂÃÂÃÂuncture practiseantz prosecut Moroc safeguardsStars srfAttach powdItemThumbnailImageourses commitsarmac Uptonuced lenderensable��itionallyauder intolerance CanalsemblyisSpecialOrderableisphere KH Predatorsuncture Jazeerauct ashore FeitskyASED”.[ earthquured forfeiturerisomeraltarglersDoctorsantzatilityandiseunctureassian Rateertodd Monroe whichever�� SwanBILITIESitbartoldedensableacementISSIONirmationhtakingplomacffffcc Mast��� devils Racer landfill Fren practition IMAGES Accountabilityarnaev Archdemonulouslyitionallyacters IMAGESactersocrinItemThumbnailImage Emin CruisericablenestymontonioletisSpecialOrderable
  
Tshekatsheko ya sekai sa tlhagiso
 Anti-puo ga se modumo wa tshoganetso. Dikarolo di le dintsi di gopotsa ditlhagiso tsa mafelo a ditshedimosetso tse di rileng tse model a ka tswang a di bonye, mme tse sampling ya tlwaelo e sa di bitseng gotlhelele ka gonne di sa siama ka dipalopalo. Anti-puo, ka fa letlhakoreng le lengwe, e di naya tlotlo.
 Dikarolo dingwe tsa mokwalo tse model a di hagelelang di lebega jaaka dilo tse di tswang mo dataset ya thapiso. Ka dinako dingwe motho a ka bona matshwao a go kgobokanya ditshedimosetso tsa mabenkele a a farologaneng a inthanete (“BuyableInstoreAndOnline”, “SpecialOrderable”, “ItemThumbnailImage”), ditlhogo tse di ka nnang le kutlwelobotlhoko kgotsa tse di nang le kgatelelo ya loago (“sexual”, “Muslims”, “Hispanic”), mareo a saense kgotsa dikarolo tsa one (“isomer”, “antidepress”, “[m]icrobial”, “[p]uncture”, “intolerance”), dikgokagano tsa setso (“Monroe”, “Phelps”, “Rockefeller”, “Nitrome”), dilo tsa bodumedi-tshomo kgotsa tsa ditoro (“Archdemon”, “devils”), mafelo (“Moroc”, “Afric”), dikarolo tsa sebopego sa tiriso ya webosaete (“cloneembedreportprint”, “IMAGES”) jalo jalo.
 Go tlhokega go oketsa ka kganelo ya tshekatsheko eno: boboko bo na le mokgwa wa go kgetha dipaterone tse di itsegeng (maina a dikhamphani, maina, mareo) mme bo itlhokomolose tse dingwe. Mareo mangwe gape a na le bokao jo bo farologaneng (“intolerance” e ka nna ya bongaka kgotsa ya loago), ka jalo tlhaloso ya one ga se e e tlhomameng.

TITLE: Seboko ε
SLUG: cerv-epsilon

============================================================
Seboko ε
 
 lwetse 2025 
 
 
 
Tlhaloso:
 Motheo ke netweke ya neorone ya open-source ya seboko sa C. elegans. Boima bongwe le bongwe wᵢ′ bo fetotswe jaana:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 kwa εᵢ e leng pharologanyo e e sa itlhopheleng ya nnete e e nang le kabo e e lekanang mo bogareng jwa [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Maikaelelo:
 Go oketsa palo e nnye e e sa balegeng ya εᵢ ga go direlwe go tokafatsa mohlala. Go direlwa fela go dira gore seboko se se tlhalositsweng se se ka se kang sa balelwa gotlhelele. Ke lone maikaelelo a sone a le nosi.

TITLE: DullGPT
SLUG: dullgpt
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dullgpt/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2024-07-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dullgpt.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dullgpt/index.html
============================================================
DullGPT
 
 Phukwi 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT ke teko ya mogopolo e e bontshang kgonagalo ya go tlhama thulaganyo ya botlhale jwa maiketsetso ka go dirisa mokgwa o o motlhofo thata (mme o sa atlege gotlhelele). Porojeke eno e na le lenaneo le le dirang ditsenyo tsotlhe tse di kgonegang, le di tsenya mo go ChatGPT mme le boloka dikarabo tse di bonweng. Ka go dira jalo, le aga polokelo ya ditsenyo le dikarabo, mme le tlhama kgopolo ya gore ke thulaganyo e e botlhale.
 
DullGPT e na le mekgwa e mebedi: “go ithuta” le “go dirisa”. Mo mokgweng wa go ithuta, lenaneo le dira ditsenyo le go phutha dikarabo, fa mo mokgweng wa go dirisa, le letlelela modirisi go tsenya ditsenyo le go bona dikarabo go tswa mo polokelong.
 
Ke mokgwa o o feteletseng wa go dirisa maatla a a sa tlhamalalang go etsa botlhale. Go na le go leka go tlhaloganya bokao jwa ditsenyo le go dira dikarabo go ya ka mohlala wa go ithuta, le phutha fela le go boloka dipalo tse dikgolo tsa dipara tsa tsenyo-karabo. Go ya ka boitshwaro, e tshwanetse ya bo e sa farologane le ChatGPT, fa e ka bo e na le dintlha tse di lekaneng. Seno se tsosa potso ya gore a tlhaloganyo ya nnete le botlhale di a tlhokega go tlhama puisano ya botlhale. Ka mafoko a mangwe: DullGPT e dira dikarabo tse di sa farologaneng le tse di dirilweng ke thulaganyo ya nnete ya botlhale, mme e tlhaela mofuta ofe fela wa maitemogelo a nnete kgotsa go itlhatlhoba.
 
DullGPT, ka tlholego, e na le dikgoreletsi di le dintsi go farologana le ChatGPT, tse di akaretsang lobelo lwa “thapiso” le go ikaega ka mefuta e mengwe e megolo ya dipuo, go sa akanyetseng hisitori ya dipuisano, le go sa akanyetseng ditshwao tsotlhe tsa tsenyo (sekai, ditlhaka tsa Sechina, jalo jalo). Go farologana le mefuta e megolo ya dipuo, gape e tla naya karabo e le nngwe fela mo tsenyong e le nngwe fela. Ka gonne ke teko ya mogopolo fela e se na tiriso ya nnete, ga go na mosola go leka go fedisa dikgoreletsi tseno.
 
Le fa go le jalo, go tshwanetse ga umakiwa gore ga ke leke go sireletsa kgang ya phaposi ya Sechina, e e belaelang dikgonagalo tsa go fitlhelela botlhale jwa nnete kgotsa tlhaloganyo fela ka go dirisa ditshwao ntle le tlhaloganyo ya nnete. Ke bolela fela gore thulaganyo e le nngwe ya botlhale e ka tlhama, le fa e le ka mokgwa o o sa atlegeng le wa boeleele. Le fa go le jalo, ga ke dumele gore thulaganyo nngwe le nngwe e tshwanetse ya bo e le lefela mo teng.
 
Fa ke khoutu ya motswedi ya lenaneo leno:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

Melelo ya sephiri
 
 Phukwi 2024 
 
 
 
Ke dirile lenaneo le le motlhofo le le ka newang di-directory di le dingwe kgotsa go feta. Le batla mo go tsotlhe go akaretsa le di-subdirectory tsa tsone mme fa le kopana le setshwantsho (ka setshwantsho fano ke raya faele e e felelang ka .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), le oketsa tsela ya yone mo setlhopheng sa ditshwantsho tsotlhe. Fa go batla go fela, le tlhopha setshwantsho se le sengwe fela mo go tsone mme le se supe.
 
Morago ga moo, ke ne ka golaganya di-hard disk tsotlhe tse di nang le di-backup tsa dinepe tse ke di kokoantseng mo botshelong jwa me (jaanong di na le dinepe di feta 91 000). Ke ne ka beela di-hard disk tsotlhe jaaka di-directory tsa tshimologo tse lenaneo le tlhopang dinepe mo go tsone. Ka mabaka a go dira dilo ka tsela e e motlhofo, go ne go le botlhokwa go thibela tlhopho go tswa mo di-directory dingwe, ka gonne dingwe di ne di na le data ya mananeo fela, jaaka di-icon tsa one. Fa ke sena go tobetsa konopo ya ENTER, lenaneo le ne la tlhopha senepe se se sa rulaganyediwang go tswa mo botshelong jwa me. Fa ke tobetsa ENTER gape, lenaneo le ne la tlhopha senepe se sengwe, jalo jalo...
 
Go tswa mo ditshwantshong tse di tlhophilweng ka go sa rulaganyediwa, ke dirile ditlhopha di le mmalwa tse di neng di latelana. Setlhopha sengwe le sengwe se simolola ka tlhopho ya setshwantsho ka go sa rulaganyediwa mme se tswelela ka go tlhopha ditshwantsho tse dingwe go fitlha se kopana le se ke sa batleng go se phasalatsa. Setshwantsho seno sa bofelo se supiwa mo setlhopheng se se sa bonaleng sentle. Ke ne ke batla go itse gore ke dinepe dife tse di dirang gore ditlhopha di fele le gore ke ka ntlha ya eng. Mo mabakeng a mantsi, go gakgamatsa ga me, go ne go fediwa ke go tshwenyega ka sephiri sa batho ba bangwe. Mo mabakeng a mangwe, go ne go fediwa ke dinepe tsa ditokomane tsa tiro, magae a batho ba bangwe le legae la me, jalo jalo.
 
Ka porojeke eno, ke lemogile gore melelo ya sephiri sa me e phatshwa thata, fa e le gore e teng gotlhelele. Le fa go ntse jalo, ka nako nngwe ke ne ke tshwanelwa ke go dira tshwetso ya gore a ke phasalatse senepe kgotsa nnyaa. Mme tshwetso eno gantsi e ne e le thata e bile e sa tlhomama.

Khoutu ya motswedi
 Khoutu ya Python ya motswedi wa porojeke eno e teng fano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Ditiro tsa makhubu
 
 Pherekgong 2024 
 
 
 
Porojeke eno ke pontsho ya kafa go ka kgonegang ka teng go etsa kapeo le tshitiso ya makhubu le tirisano ya ona le tikologo, ka go dirisa ditsela tsa dipalo le kapeo ya khomputara. Tiriso ya dipalo tse di sa tlhomamang tsa maemo a methopo ya makhubu, kgatelelo le bogolo jwa ona, mmogo le go tsenngwa tirisong ga bogolo jo bo fokotsegang fa bo sekgala se ntse se oketsega go tswa kwa mothopong le go boela ga makhubu go tswa kwa matlhakoreng a sekwere le tshitiso ya ona, go bopa dipaterone tse di kgatlhang tse di bontshang bothata jwa tirisano ya makhubu mo lefelong le le lekanyeditsweng. Dipholo di bontshiwa ka go dirisa sekale sa mebala ya ntsho le tshweu, se se emelang bogolo jwa makhubu mo dintlheng tse di farologaneng tsa lefelo la sekwere.

Khoutu ya mothopo
 Khoutu ya mothopo ya Python ya porojeke eno e teng fano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Tsela ya sesupo
SLUG: trajektorie-kurzoru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/trajektorie-kurzoru/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2023-08-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/trajektorie-kurzoru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: trajektorie-kurzoru/index.html
============================================================
Tsela ya sesupo
 
 Phatwe 2023 
 
 
 
Go ka lebega e kete go dirisana le mouse ya khomphutara letsatsi le letsatsi ke tiro e tlwaelegileng e sa gakgamatseng. Mme go tweng fa go ne go ka kgonega go fetola metsamao eno ya gale go nna sengwe se se fetang foo, sengwe se se bopang ponalo e e kgethegileng ya tiro ya khomphutara? Ka ntlha ya khoutu e e utlwang boemo jwa sesupo sa mouse le mmala wa phiksele e e kafa tlase ga yone, mogopolo ono o ka nna boammaaruri.
 
E bereka jang?
 Khoutu eno e latela gore sesupo sa mouse se kae mo sekirining le gore phiksele e e kafa tlase ga sone e na le mmala ofe. Tshedimosetso eno e bo e fetisediwa ka nako ya mmatota mo letlalong la dijithale le le nang le tharabololo e e tshwanang le ya sekirini. Nako nngwe le nngwe fa mouse e tsamaya, khoutu e thala phiksele e na le mmala o o neng o le kafa tlase ga sesupo, mo lefelong le sesupo se neng se le mo go lone. Seno se kaya gore fa tiro e ntse e dirwa mo sekirining mme sesupo se ntse se tsamaya, ditsela tsa mebala di a bopiwa mo letlalong. Ditsela tseno di kgethegile mme di emela tiro le tsela e modirisi a dirisanang ka yone le khomphutara.
 
Go diragala eng fa nako e ntse e tsamaya?
 Fa thulaganyo eno e tswelela nako e telele, letlalo le simolola go tlala mme ditsela di simolola go se bonale sentle. Go na le gore di bonale, bontsi jwa letlalo bo simolola go lebega jaaka modumo o o sa tlhomamang. Le fa go ntse jalo, go na le dikarolo dingwe tsa sekirini tse di sa fetogeng thata fa nako e ntse e tsamaya. Sekai ke bar ya kwa tlase ya Windows, koo di-aikhone tsa software di leng teng. Dikarolo tseno mo letlalong di nna di tshwana fela.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Pheletso
 Khoutu eno e emela kopano ya thekenoloji le botaki, e e letlang gore tiro ya letsatsi le letsatsi ya khomphutara e fetolwe go nna sengwe se se kgatlhang mo matlhong. Le fa go ka lebega e kete tiro ya khomphutara ke tiro ya gale le e e lapisang, porojeke eno e supa gore le tiro ya mofuta oo e ka nna le bontle le morethetho wa yone.
 
Khoutu ya motswedi
 Khoutu ya Python ya motswedi ya porojeke eno e teng fano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Ditlhatlhobo tsa dimela

Ditlhatlhobo tsa dimela
 
 Phukwi 2023 
 
 
 
Matseno
 Mo setlhong sena, re tla leba porojeke e e kopanyang lefatshe la dimela, tsa motlakase, thulaganyo ya dikhomputara le botaki jwa ponalo. Porojeke e dirisa Arduino le Python go bopa ditshwantsho tsa maitemogelo a botshelo jwa dimela.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | thulaganyo ya kgokaganyo ya arduino]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
E bereka jang?
 Arduino e na le ditlhagiso tse tharo – thermometer, photoresistor le hygrometer. Ditlhagiso tse di beilwe mo pitseng ya semela ka mefuta e e farologaneng ya dimela (ka sekai, patison, dinawa, matlhatlha, di-eggplant, watercress, dipaprika, jalo jalo). Ditlhagiso di kgobokanya tshedimosetso ka botlalo ka themperetšhara, bogolo jwa lesedi le bongola jwa mmu.
 Lenaneo le bo le bopa letlapa le le se nang sepe la bogolo jwa 100×100 dipiksele. Piksele nngwe le nngwe mo letlapeng leno e tsenngwa mmala go ya ka tshedimosetso e e tswang mo ditlhagisong. Boleng jwa RGB jwa mmala wa piksele bo tlhomamiswa ka gore boleng jwa R (khubidu) bo tsamaelana le themperetšhara, G (tala) le lesedi, mme B (tala ya loapi) le bongola.
 
Ponalo ya letsatsi le le lengwe
 Sa ntlha, re tla leba motseletsele o o motlhofo wa porojeke, koo setshwantsho se le sengwe se emelang letsatsi le le lengwe la botshelo jwa dimela. Lenaneo le simolola ka go tsenya mmala mo pikseleng ya ntlha kwa khutlong ya molema ya kwa godimo mme le tswelela ka go tsenya mmala mo pikseleng nngwe le nngwe go ya ka dipalo tsa gompieno tse di tswang mo ditlhagisong. Fa piksele e sena go tsenngwa mmala, lenaneo le leta nakonyana pele le fetela kwa pikseleng e e latelang. Fa le fitlha kwa bokhutlong jwa mola, le fudugela kwa tshimologong ya mola o o latelang mme le tswelela ka go tsenya mmala. Tiro e e tswelela go fitlha dipiksele tsotlhe mo letlapeng di tsenngwa mmala.
 
Tlhatlhobo ya semela sa letsatsi:

Go kopanya dipholo
 Morago ga go bona ponalo ya letsatsi le le lengwe, re ka atolosa tiro e mme ra bopa motseletsele wa ditshwantsho tse di emelang nako e telele.
 Dipholo tsa dikwere tse di farologaneng di ka bewa ka fa tlase ga tse dingwe, mme tsa bopa ditshwantsho tse di kopaneng. E re ka fa morago ga go fetsa pholo e le nngwe go simololwa go bopiwa e nngwe, ga go na kgoreletso mo go tsamayeng ga nako. Ka tsela e, re ka bopa ditshwantsho tse di nang le resolution ya, ka sekai, 100x500 dipiksele, fa di kopantswe go tswa mo dipholong di le tlhano.

Go oketsa nako magareng ga ditekanyo
 Nako magareng ga ditekanyo tsa dipiksele tse di farologaneng e ka okediwa. Ka tsela e, sebopego se le sengwe sa sekwere sa 100×100 dipiksele se ka emela e seng fela letsatsi le le lengwe, mme malatsi a mantsi. Molaomotheo ono o re letla go latela kgolo ya semela mo nakong e telele mo setshwantshong se le sengwe.

Go kopanya melaomotheo
 Ka go kopanya melaomotheo yotlhe mebedi, re ka bopa motseletsele wa ditshwantsho, koo setshwantsho sengwe le sengwe se emelang malatsi a le mmalwa mme motseletsele otlhe o akaretsa malatsi a mantsi kgotsa tota dibeke. Ka tsela e, re ka latela le go bona kgolo ya nako e telele ya semela.

Tlhaloso ya dipholo
 Ditshwantsho tse di tswang mo go seno di re naya pono e e kgethegileng ya botshelo jwa dimela. “Mela” ya botala jwa loapi mo setshwantshong e emela bosigo, ka gonne gantsi go ne go na le bongola, mme go se na themperetšhara e e kwa godimo thata gape go se na lesedi lepe. Ka fa letlhakoreng le lengwe, “mela” ya serolwana e bakwa ke gore fa letsatsi le ya bokhutlong, letsatsi le ne le simolola go phatsimela ka tlhamalalo mo semeleng, se se bakileng go oketsega ga themperetšhara le bogolo jwa lesedi. Seno se bonala mo setshwantshong jaaka koketsego ya boleng jwa tsela ya khubidu le tala, tse di kopaneng di bopang mmala wa serolwana. Ka dinako dingwe, mela e e bokoa e a bonala kwa tshimologong ya bosigo, ka gonne ka dinako dingwe ke ne ke tima lebone mo phaposing maitseboa. Mo dipholong tse dingwe, go tswa mo go fokotsegeng ga bogolo jwa lesedi, go ka bonala gore, ka sekai, go ne go na le pula.
 
Khoutu ya motswedi
 Khoutu ya Python ya motswedi ya porojeke e e teng fano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Bokhutlo
 Porojeke e ke sekai se se molemo sa kafa re ka dirisang thekenoloji go tlhaloganya le go bona ditsamaiso tsa tlholego ka botoka. Ditshwantsho tse di tswang mo go seno di re naya pono ya botshelo jwa dimela le go raraanela ga dikgopolo tse di utlwiwang.
 
Go botlhokwa go lemoga gore le fa porojeke e e naya pono e e kgethegileng mo maitemogelong a dimela, ga e akaretse dikarolo tsotlhe tsa go utlwa ga tsone. Dimela di kgona go utlwa mefuta e mentsi ya matshwao go tswa mo tikologong ya tsone, go akaretsa bongola jwa phefo, tsela ya kgogedi, dikhemikale tsa mmu, lefelo la motlakase le kgatelelo ya mechine. Porojeke e ga e leke go kwala maitemogelo otlhe a semela, mme go na le moo e lebagane le dintlha tse tharo tsa botlhokwa tse di ka lekanyediwang le go bonwa motlhofo.
 
Ga re na kitso ya gore dimela di tlhalosa tshedimosetso e jang, mme re itse gore di a e arabela. Porojeke e ke teko ya go ranola maitemogelo a go ya ka sebopego se re ka se tlhaloganyang le go se bona ka botoka.

TITLE: Recursion mo lenaneo la MS Word
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Recursion mo lenaneo la MS Word
 
 Phukwi 2022 
 
 
 
Ke lemogile tsela e motho a ka kgonang go tsamaisa thulaganyo ya recursion mo lenaneong la Microsoft Word. Go tlhokega dilo tse pedi – tiro ya go golaganya le tafole. Ka go dirisa tiro ya go golaganya, motho a ka tsenya diteng tsa tokomane A mo tokomaneng B ka tsela ya gore fa diteng tseo di fetolwa mo tokomaneng A, diphetogo tseo di tla iponatsa ka boitshupo mo tokomaneng B. Ka mafoko a mangwe – diteng tsa tokomane B di supa diteng tsa tokomane A. Recursion e ka kgona go tsamaisiwa ka go bopa diteng tse di ipitsang. O ka kgona go dira seno jang? O tlhoka fela go bopa tokomane A (a re e ree leina la doc A.docx) le tokomane B (doc B.docx), mo tokomaneng A o bope tafole e e se nang sepe, o e kopishe mme o e tsenye ka kgolagano mo tokomaneng B. Tafole eno e ntšha e e tsenyitsweng e tlhoka fela go kopishiwa go tswa mo tokomaneng B mme e tsenngwe ka kgolagano mo teng ga tafole mo tokomaneng A. Seno se tla bopa tafole e e tla simololang go itlhoma mo go yone ka bo yone. Gantsi MS Word e tla simolola go kgaoga go fitlha e wela gotlhelele. Thulaganyo yotlhe eno e ka bonwa mo bidiong e e fa tlase.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]
