TITLE: Kgopolo ya phapang ya mela e emeng le e rapameng
SLUG: iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-08-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu/index.html
============================================================
Kgopolo ya phapang ya mela e emeng le e rapameng
 
 Phatwe 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-1.jpg | papadišo ya mela e emeng le e rapameng]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Mela e rapameng mo setshwantshong se se fa godimo e lebega e se na phapang e ntsi fa o e leba go tswa kgakala go na le mela e emeng. Mme tota, di a tshwana, jaaka go ka bonala fa o leba gaufi. Fa o sekamisa tlhogo go ya molemeng kgotsa go ya mojeng ka 45°, kgopolo eno e a nyelela. Fa o sekamisa tlhogo go ya molemeng kgotsa go ya mojeng ka 90°, mela e e rapameng e fetoga go nna e emeng, mme ka jalo e lebega e na le phapang e ntsi. Ke lemogile kgopolo eno mo setshwantshong sa spiral ya Fibonacci ya lefoko [https://vojtamaur.cz/spirala-fibonacciho-slova/]. Setshwantsho seno se se sekwere se lebega jaaka e kete se dirilwe ka dikhutlotharo di le nnè - tse pedi di tletse ka mela e rapameng mme tse pedi di tletse ka mela e emeng. Ke dumela gore kgopolo eno e bereka ka molaotheo o o tshwanang le wa kgopolo ya mela e emeng le e rapameng [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vertical%E2%80%93horizontal_illusion]. Gape ke dumela gore seno se amana le tsela e ditlhopha dingwe tsa di-neuron di arabelang ka yone mo dikgothatsong tse di farologaneng. Di-neuron di arabela ka tsela e e farologaneng mo dikgothatsong tse di farologaneng jaaka mela e emeng, mela ya diagonal, mela e rapameng, jalo jalo (bona buka ya Gödel, Escher, Bach, kgaolo 11). Se se gakgamatsang ke gore kgopolo eno e ama mongwe le mongwe ka tsela e e farologaneng - bangwe ba e bona sentle thata mme bangwe ga ba e bone gotlhelele. Kgopolo eno e bonala thata le go feta mo setshwantshong se se fa tlase.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-2.jpg | setshwantsho se se dirilweng ka mela e emeng le e rapameng]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
TITLE: Sefalane sa lefoko la Fibonacci
SLUG: spirala-fibonacciho-slova
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/spirala-fibonacciho-slova/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-08-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/spirala-fibonacciho-slova.mdx
BUILT_HTML: spirala-fibonacciho-slova/index.html
============================================================
Sefalane sa lefoko la Fibonacci
 
 phatwe 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg | Sefalane sa lefoko la Fibonacci: tlhagiso ya lenaneo la ga Pavel Burda]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Go thala sebopego se, ke tsentshitse lefoko la Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_slovo] mo sefalaneng ka tsela e e tshwanang le ya go tsenya dipalo tsa tlholego mo sefalaneng sa Ulam. Ke tshwaile dipalo tse di farologaneng mo sefalaneng se go ya ka molao: 0 = sekwere se se ntsho, 1 = sekwere se se sweu (tsamaiso e e bontshitswe mo tshikinyegong e e fa tlase). Go dira gore tsamaiso e nne e e nang le bokgoni, go dirisitswe puo ya go kwala dikhodo ya JavaScript.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif | tshikinyego e e bontshang tsamaiso]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif] 
 
Lefoko la Fibonacci ke eng?
 Lefoko la Fibonacci ke tatelano e e kgethegileng ya dipalo tsa binary (kgotsa ka bophara, matshwao afe fela a mabedi). Re e bona ka tsela e e tshwanang le tatelano ya Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_posloupnost], fela mo boemong jwa go thopha re dirisa go kopanya mafoko. Ditho tse pedi tsa ntlha di tlhalositswe jaana: S0 = 0, S1 = 01. Setho se se latelang se se tlhagisitsweng ka go kopanya (ke gore, go oketsa setho sa bobedi go tloga kwa bofelong morago ga setho sa bofelo) e tla nna S2 = 010. Ka jalo, tatelano ya dipalo di se kae tsa ntlha go tswa mo mafokong a Fibonacci e tla lebega jaana: 0100101001001010010100100101001001…
 
Sefalane sa Ulam ke eng?
 Sefalane sa Ulam [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ulamova_spir%C3%A1la], kgotsa sefalane sa dipalo tsa ntlha, ke setshwantsho se se tlhagiswang ka go rulaganya dipalo tsa tlholego mo sefalaneng le go supa dipalo tsa ntlha. Se ribolotswe ke ra-dipalo Stanisław Ulam ka ngwaga wa 1963. Ulam o ne a kwala dipalo tsa tlholego mo letloweng la khutlonnetsepa, a baya nngwe mo bogareng mme a baya dipalo tse dingwe ka tsela ya sefalane go ya kwa ntle.
 
Khodo ya motswedi ya porojeke
 
[CODE BLOCK]
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Spirála Fibonacciho slova</title>

  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">

  <style>
    #container {
      text-align: center;
      position: absolute;
      left: 50%;
      top: 50%;
      padding: 20px;
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      width: 600px;
      height: 600px;
      background-color: white;
    }

    #point {
      color: black;
      background-color: white;
      border:none;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }

    #point2 {
      color: white;
      background-color: black;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      border:none;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main id='container'>
  </main>

  <script>
    /* FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    function spiral(n) {
      if (n === 0) return [0, 0, r];
      --n;

      var r = Math.floor((Math.sqrt(n + 1) - 1) / 2) + 1;
      var p = (8 * r * (r - 1)) / 2;
      var en = r * 2;
      var a = (1 + n - p) % (r * 8);
      var pos = [0, 0, r];

      switch (Math.floor(a / (r * 2))) {
        case 0:
          {
            pos[0] = a - r;
            pos[1] = -r;
          }
          break;
        case 1:
          {
            pos[0] = r;
            pos[1] = (a % en) - r;
          }
          break;
        case 2:
          {
            pos[0] = r - (a % en);
            pos[1] = r;
          }
          break;
        case 3:
          {
            pos[0] = -r;
            pos[1] = r - (a % en);
          }
          break;
      }

      return pos;
    }

    /* FIBONACCIHO SLOVA */
    container = document.getElementById("container");
    var var0 = "<div id='point'></div>";
    var var1 = "<div id='point2'></div>";
    var var3;
    var text = "";
    var i;

    /* ITERACE NEFUNGUJE PO JEDNOM CTVERECKU ALE PO KAZDEM SOUCTU JEDNICEK A NUL */
    for (var i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
      var3 = var0 + var1;
      var1 = var0;
      var0 = var3;

      text += var3;
      container.innerHTML = text;
    }

    /* VOLANI FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    var cislo = document.getElementById('container').getElementsByTagName("div");

    for(var i=0; i<cislo.length; i++) {
      cislo[i].setAttribute("class", "jedno");

      var pos = spiral(i);

      cislo[i].style.right = 300 + 40 * pos[0] + "px";
      cislo[i].style.top = 300 + 40 * pos[1] + "px";
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

TITLE: Mmala, sebope le modumo
SLUG: barva-tvar-ton
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barva-tvar-ton/
LANGUAGE: tn

DATE: 2019-06-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barva-tvar-ton.mdx

Mmala, sebope le modumo
 
 khubedu 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png | thuto ya mebala - Wassily Kandinsky]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png] 
 
Go ya ka moitseanape Wassily Kandinsky [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasilij_Kandinskij] le thuto ya gagwe ya mebala, go na le kamano magareng ga mmala le sebopego. Mo modelong ya mebala ya RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] e e neng e dirisiwa ka nako eo (khubedu, serolwana, botala jwa loapi), o ne a abela dibopego di le tharo tsa dipalopalo2D mo kamanong e e latelang: khutlotharo = serolwana, khutlonne = khubedu, sediko = botala jwa loapi. Ke tlhotlheleditswe ke tiro ya gagwe mme ka tlhama phetolelo ya segompieno ya thuto eno ya mebala. Go na le modela wa mebala wa RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] o o ka tswang o tsofala gompieno, ke dirisitse modela wa mebala wa RGB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGB] o o tlhomameng thata ka fa molaong wa fisiki e bile o dirisiwa thata gompieno (khubedu, botala jwa matlhare, botala jwa loapi). Khutlonne e Kandinsky a e dirisitseng ke e emiseditse ka khutlothano, ka gonne khutlonne e tshwana thata le khutlotharo go na le sediko, fa khutlothano yona e nkgopotsa phetoho e e tshwanetseng magareng ga khutlotharo le sediko.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png | thuto ya mebala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png] 
 
Go batla sebopego se se tshwanetseng (khutlothano) magareng ga khutlotharo le sediko, ke dirisitse dikhutlo tsa ka fa gare tsa dibopego. Dikhutlo tsa ka fa gare tsa khutlotharo ya mahlakore a a lekanang ke 60° (kgotsa 60^1). Dikhutlo tsa ka fa gare tsa sediko di tshwanetse go nna 180° (kgotsa 60^1.268324336648371). Ee, sediko ga se na mahlakore, ka jalo ga se kake sa nna le dikhutlo tsa ka fa gare, mme ka sekai, dikhutlo tsa ka fa gare tsa sebopego sa mahlakore a le sekete ke 179,64° mme sebopego seno se lebega thata jaaka sediko. Fa ke bala palogare ya di-exponent tseno tse pedi 60^[(1+1.268324336648371):2] ke bona dipholo tsa 103.923°. Dikhutlo tseno tsa ka fa gare di tshwana thata le tsa khutlothano, e dikhutlo tsa yona tsa ka fa gare e leng 108°. Go atolosa thuto eno ya mebala, ke dirisitse mokgwa o o tshwanang go oketsa diphetoho magareng ga dibopego tsa motheo tsa mebala ka dikamano tse di latelang: khutlonne = serolwana, khutloseswai = cyan. Ke lekile go tlatsa thuto eno ka go abela dibopego tsa mebala go ya ka bogodimo jwa modumo go ya ka kamano: khutlo e e bogale + mmala o o bothitho = modumo o o kwa godimo, khutlo e e sa bogaleng + mmala o o tsididi = modumo o o kwa tlase. Ke kwadile kgopolo eno mo setlhopheng sa dinoto.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png | thuto e e atoloseditsweng ya mebala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png] 
 
Ke lekile go tlatsa thuto eno ka go abela dibopego tsa mebala go ya ka bogodimo jwa modumo go ya ka kamano: khutlo e e bogale + mmala o o bothitho = modumo o o kwa godimo, khutlo e e sa bogaleng + mmala o o tsididi = modumo o o kwa tlase. Ke kwadile kgopolo eno mo setlhopheng sa dinoto.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg | thuto ya mmino wa mebala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Mmala o le mongwe fela o ke sa kgonang go o abela sebopego kgotsa modumo ke magenta. O tshwanetse go tshwana le botala jwa matlhare (le ona o magareng ga botala jwa loapi le khubedu gape o tsididi e bile o bothitho ka nako e le nngwe). A o tshwanetse go lebega jaaka halofo ya sediko? Kgotsa jaaka khutlotharo e e dikhutlo di borethe? Kgotsa a o tshwanetse go nna o le mongwe fela le botala jwa matlhare - ka jalo gape e le khutlothano? Go fitlha jaanong ga ke ise ke bone karabo ya potso eno.

TITLE: Pono ya motho
SLUG: lidsky-zrak
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lidsky-zrak/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lidsky-zrak.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lidsky-zrak/index.html
============================================================
Pono ya motho
 
 phaphama 2019 
 
 
 
Pono ya motho e fapane tota le se batho ba bangwe ba se akanyang. Dintlha tsa ntlha tse di dirwang ke matlho a rona di farologane thata le kafa boboko bo di ranolang ka teng, mme ka jalo le kafa re akanyang gore re bona ka teng. Setshwantsho se se fa tlase se supa "dintlha tse di phepa" tse di sa tlhakantshwang le "post-production" ya boboko jwa rona.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png | pono ya pono ya motho]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png] 
 
Mo setshwantshong seno, motho a ka lemoga diphoso di le mmalwa tse di bonalang:
 
 
- Setshwantsho se retologetswe ka fa morago ka tsela ya seipone ka go rapama le ka go tlhamalala. Ngwana mongwe le mongwe o bona jaana fa a sena go tsholwa, mme fa nako e ntse e tsamaya boboko bo ithuta go retolola setshwantsho. Phelelo eno gape e ka direga ka sewelo morago ga go nwa di-hallucinogen tse di kgaoganyang maikutlo.
 
- Setshwantsho se supiwa ka go farologana mo leitlhong lengwe le lengwe - boboko ke jone fela bo se kopanyang go nna setshwantsho se le sengwe.
 
- Setshwantsho se bogale fela mo karolong e nnye ya mo gare mme se a fifala fa se ya kwa mahlakoreng.
 
- Setshwantsho se na le mebala e mentsi mo karolong e nnye ya mo gare mme se latlhegelwa ke go nna le mmala o o tseneletseng fa se ya kwa mahlakoreng. Seno se bakwa ke palo e ntsi ya di-cone mo gare ga leitlho, e e fokotsegang ka bonako fa e tswa mo gare. Mo leitlhong lengwe le lengwe go na le lerothodi le lentsho le le lengwe - le le bidiwang sebaka se se sa boneng (ka botsebi se bidiwa scotoma). Mo karolong eno ya leitlho ga go na di-cone kgotsa di-rod, ka gonne nchwe ya leitlho e mo karolong eo. Boboko gantsi bo tlatsa dibaka tseno tse di sa boneng ka dintlha tse di tswang mo leitlhong le lengwe.
 
- Mela e e tlhamaletseng e kgaogantswe go nna dikarolo tsa sediko jaaka e kete ka phello ya fisheye kgotsa ka lens ya khamera ya bophara. Boboko bo leka go tlhamalatsa mela eno, mme ke maaka fela.
 
- Nko le yone e a bonala mo lefelong la pono, e boboko bo tlwaetseng go e tlhotlha.
 
- Setshwantsho ga se phepa gotlhelele, mme se na le modumo o monnye wa pono. Seno se bopiwa ke motlhakase wa modumo wa lesedi, modumo wa di-photoreceptor ka botsone le ditiro tse dingwe tsa nchwe tsa tsamaiso ya pono. Mo maemong a tlwaelegileng, boboko bo bo gatelela thata, mme bo ka bonala thata mo lefifing, fa matlho a tswetswe kgotsa mo mafelong a a boreledi.
 
 
Mo tirong ya me ya bofelo ya ngwaga wa bobedi wa thuto ya multimedia mo thutong ya animation kwa Sekolong sa Kgwebo, Botaki le Tlhamo, ke dirile bidio e e etshedisang mathata a a farologaneng a matlho a a kgatlhang:
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/sTMWRaVGFIE]

Tanzania 2019
 
Ditshwantsho, dikwalo
 
 
 
Tlhagiso eno ke bokhutshwane jwa ditshwaelo le dikitso tse di farologaneng tse ke di kokoanyeng ka nako ya fa ke ne ke le mo Tanzania ka nakwana. Ke supa ditshwaelo le maikutlo a ka a ka ditshwantsho le ka dikwalo.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Ke leka go thusa baagi ba koo ka tiro ya me, go anama kitso ka mathata a bone le go fedisa tlhaolele ya morafe. Gape ke dumela gore Yuropa e tshwanetse go ithuta mo lefatsheng leno mo dilong di le dintsi.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
 
Mo Tanzania go na le ditlhopha tse pedi tse dikgolo tsa bodumedi - Islam le Bokeresete. Le fa go nna mmogo ga ditlhopha tse pedi tse go ka nna ga se ka ga tlhaloganyesega sentle mo moagi wa Yuropa wa lekgolo la 21 la dingwaga, Mamosleme le Bakeresete ba koo ba tshela ka kagiso le go tlotlana.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg] 

 
Ka namana ga ke rate batho ba ba apereng yunifomo (mapodisi, masole, badiredi ba tshireletso...). Gongwe fela mo Czech Republic. Ke dumela gore ka dithata tse ba di neilweng, boikgantsho jwa bone bo a gola. Le fa go ntse jalo, kgatlhanong ya me le yunifomo e ne e se yone gotlhelele mo Tanzania opodstatněný.Lid ba ba apereng yunifomo koo ba ne ba ntsaya sentle e bile ba nthusitse ka nako tsotlhe.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]

Baagi ba koo ba ntsaya e le batho ba ba amogelang baeng le ditsala, le fa e le kwa ntle ga mafelo a bojanala. Fa ke etetse motse wa koo, batho ba ne ba nkamogela ka tumediso ya "Jambo!" (e e rayang "Dumela!"). Bontsi jwa bone ba ne ba buisana le nna.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]

"Batho ba ba tswang kwa mafelong a a mogote ba, ba ntlhabela leitlho ba na le mowa wa go nyenya go gaisa rona" (karolo ya motlotlo wa baeti ba bangwe o ke o utlwileng mo sefofaneng fa ke boela kwa Czech Republic)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Ka ntlha ya kgwebisano ya lefatshe lotlhe, bojanala bo a gola kwa Zanzibar, bo tisa melemo e mentsi, mme gape le mathata. Melemo e megolo ke kgolo ya ikonomi le go oketsega ga maemo a thuto. Molemo o mongwe ke go oketsega ga kitso ka lefatshe leno mo lefatsheng, mme ka maswabi molemo o, gantsi o fedisiwa ke mabota a magolo a dihotele a baeti ba le bantsi ba sa a kgabaganyeng. Ke tsaya go senngwa ga ngwao ya koo e le mathata. Sekai sa mathata a ke go fuduga ga Masai a le mantsi go tswa kwa Tanzania (segolo thata go tswa kgaolong ya Kilimanjaro) go ya kwa mafelong a a nang le baeti ba le bantsi (kwa mabopong a Zanzibar). Masai ba, ba rekisa dilwana koo mme ba nna fela jaaka kgatlhego ya bojanala. Masai ba dumela gore fa mongwe a ba tsaya ditshwantsho, o utswa moya wa bone. Le fa go ntse jalo, mo mafelong a bojanala, ba itumelela go tsaya ditshwantsho ka madi a mannye.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

TITLE: Setshwantsho sa motho
SLUG: portret-osobnosti
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/portret-osobnosti/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/portret-osobnosti.mdx
BUILT_HTML: portret-osobnosti/index.html
============================================================
Setshwantsho sa motho
 
 Morule 2018 
 
 
 
Porojeke eno e tlhamilwe jaaka tiro ya sekolo ya thuto ya Dinepe kwa Sekolong se Segolo sa Kgwebo, Botaki jo bo Dirisiwang le Tlhabololo kwa Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Maikaelelo e ne e le go kopanya ka tsela ya diteko dinepe tsa ditshwantsho tsa batho, mefuta ya semelo sa tlhaloganyo, kgatiso ya ditshwantsho, tlhagiso ya metshikinyego le modumo. Popego ya bofelo ya dinepe le dibidio e ne e ikaegile ka teko ya semelo ya Eysenck le ka kgaoganyo ya mefuta ya semelo go ya ka tsela ya ga Galen.

TITLE: Rastara ya tšheke
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Rastara ya tšheke
 
 Moranang 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Ke dirisitse rastara ya tšheke mo senepe sa pontsho, moo tšheke nngwe le nngwe e lekanang le piksele e le nngwe ya senepe. Mafelo a a tšhweu a tšheke ke dipiksele tsa ntlha tsa senepe fa rastara eno e sena go dirisiwa, mme mafelo a a ntsho ke dipiksele tse di fetotsweng mmala le lesedi. Senepe se se tletseng se lebega jaaka lefelo le le imi e le nngwe ya mmala o o imi, mme fa o se atametsa ka dijitale, senepe sa ntlha se a bonala. Gore se bonale sentle mo monitorong ya Full HD, senepe se tshwanetse go nna le boleele jwa dikhono tse di ka nnang 2000 px. Go ka nna ga nna le diphaposi fa o bula senepe mo sebatling se se fetolang bogolo jwa marang a dipiksele ka tsela ya bicubic. Sebatli se se tlhomamisitsweng ke se se tshwanang le Windows Photo Viewer.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Jaaka sekai se sengwe sa tiriso ya molaomotheo ono, ke bopile senepe se se nang le phapano ya mmala le ya bokao.

============================================================
Go sothafala ga tshedimosetso
 
 Phatwe 2018 
 
 
 
Ke ka baemedi ba le kae ba ba fetisang setlhogo se re se bonang mo interneteng go fitlha kwa go rona? A boammaaruri jwa sone bo a tshepega? Lefatshe le re le bonang ka dikhuta tsa rona tsa megala, dikhomphutara le ditelebishene le farologane go le kana kang?
 
Go botlhokwa go netefatsa tshedimosetso nngwe le nngwe e re e bonang go tswa mo methopong e le mmalwa mme re se ka ra itlhagela go dumela gore ke boammaaruri.
 
Tiro eno e dirilwe ka go tsaya senepe se le sengwe mme morago ga moo ya bo e le go se tsaya gape ka makgetlo a le mantsi go tswa mo seiponeng sa khomphutara.

TITLE: Meribo e mebedi
SLUG: dva-rytmy
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dva-rytmy/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dva-rytmy.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dva-rytmy/index.html
============================================================
Meribo e mebedi
 
 Moranang 2018 
 
 
 
Bidio ya ntlha e bopilwe ka meribo e mebedi: go phatsima ga pono le go tlhanya ga modumo. Meribo e mebedi e e na le nako e e sa lekaneng sentle. Ka phapogonyana e nnye e, bidio e fitlhelela maikutlo a a seng monate mo karolong nngwe. Mo bidiong ya bobedi, ke okeditse ka ditshwantsho tse di malebana le kgatlhanong le ntwa mo mogopolong o o motheo.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/X9ms8pp89Pg?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg | mogopolo o o motheo]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg] 
Meribo e mebedi 1 - ntsho x tsheu, mogopolo o o motheo
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MQIDzwTYjoA?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg | tiriso ya mogopolo o o motheo]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg] 
Meribo e mebedi 2 - tiriso ya mogopolo o o motheo mo ditshwantshong

TITLE: Dipalo tse di sa rulaganang tse di tlhophilweng ke motho
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Dipalo tse di sa rulaganang tse di tlhophilweng ke motho
 
 Moranang 2018 
 
 
 
Mo tirong eno, go ne ga abelwa batho ba le mmalwa letlhare la dipotso. Mo letlhareng leno la dipotso, batho ba ba neng ba lekwa ba ne ba kwala dipalo di le lekgolo tse di sa rulaganang mo gare ga 1-100, tse di neng tsa tla mo mogopolong wa bone. Morago ga moo, ke ne ka fetisetsa dipalo tseno mo khomputareng mme ka dira kerafo e le nngwe go tswa mo letlhareng lengwe le lengwe la dipotso. Lekeletso ya x e supa tatelano ya dipalo ka bongwe mme lekeletso ya y e supa boleng jwa dipalo tse di tlhophilweng. Kerafo ya mmala o o botala jwa legodimo e dirilwe ke khomputara, ke gore, ke jenereitara ya dipalo tse di sa rulaganang. Fa o bapisa dikerafo tsotlhe, o ka lemoga gore motho mongwe le mongwe yo o lekilweng o na le kerafo e e kgethegileng e e nang le mokgwa wa yone wa go tsenya dipalo. Gape go ka lemogwa gore mo dikerafong di le dintsi go na le dipotologo dingwe tse di batlang di tshwana (gantsi ka tsela ya go oketsa le go fokotsa dipalo ka dikgato).
 
DIPALO TSE DI TLHOPHILWENG KE JENEREITARA YA DIPALO TSE DI SA RULAGANANG
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg | Kerafo ya dipalo tse di sa rulaganang tse di tlhophilweng ke khomputara]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
DIPALO TSE DI TLHOPHILWENG KE BATHO

SLUG: nepravidelny-dech
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nepravidelny-dech/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nepravidelny-dech.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nepravidelny-dech/index.html
============================================================
Phefumoloho e e sa tlhomamang
 
 Tlhakole 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Mo tseleng eno ya modumo ya metsotso e mene go na le rekoto ya katse e e swang. Mo rekotong eno go utlwala medumo e mebedi e megolo: phefumoloho e e sa tlhomamang le modumo o o sa utlwaleng thata wa go itaya ga pelo.

============================================================
TITLE: Go tswa mo ponong ya kgapetla
SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Go tswa mo ponong ya kgapetla
 
 Tlhakole 2018 
 
 
 
Dibidio tse di tsepamisitse mogopolo mo tseleng e kgapetla e lemogang ka yone, gore e ikutlwa jang le gore e itemogela eng? Pono ya yone ya lefatshe ga e botlhokwa go feta ya motho! Mo bidiong ya ntlha, motho a ka bona rekoto ya ntlhantlha ya lewatle. Mo bidiong ya bobedi, mmala o o tlwaelegileng wa ditshwantsho tse di farologaneng tsa rekoto ya ntlhantlha o bontshiwa mo letlhakoreng la molema la sekirini. Mo letlhakoreng la moja la sekirini go na le kerafo ya RGB e e tlhalosang mmala ono.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

TITLE: Botlho
SLUG: plisen
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/plisen/

DATE: 2017-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/plisen.mdx
BUILT_HTML: plisen/index.html
============================================================
Botlho
 
 lapologo 2017 
 
 
 
Mokgwa ono wa dinepe o supa botlho mo bukeng ya me ya puo ya Sejeremane. Dinepe di rulagantswe ka tatelano ya ditsebe tsa buka. Ka tsela e, dinepe nngwe le nngwe e nna le dikomposishene tse di sa rulaganyetswang, tse di kgatlhang le tsa diteko.

TITLE: Go tlhakatlhakanya raster e e tlwaelegileng
SLUG: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/

============================================================
Go tlhakatlhakanya raster e e tlwaelegileng
 
 Diphalane 2017 
 
 
 
Mo tirong eno, ke lekile go bopa letsholo la ditiro tsa botaki tsa tlhaloganyo. Ke kgaogantse tokomane eno ka dikarolo di le nne. Mo karolong ya ntlha, ke bopile raster ya motheo e e tlwaelegileng, mo karolong ya bobedi ke tsweletse go tswa mo rastereng eno mme ka simolola go e tlhakatlhakanya ka bonya mme ka dira jalo ka baka go se itumele. Mo karolong ya boraro, ke tlhakatlhakantse raster eno ya geometrike e e tlwaelegileng ka dibopego tsa tlhago tse di sa tlwaelegang tse di farologaneng. Mme mo karolong ya bofelo ya bone, ke dirile gore dibopego tseno tsa tlhago di tsamae.
 
Karolo ya ntlha:
 RASTER YA MOTHEO
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Tlhaloganyo 1.0 - raster e e tlwaelegileng]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
Karolo ya bobedi:
 GO TSHWENYA RASTER KA PHAPOGO E POTLANA

Tsogo
 
  Moranang 2017 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-1.jpg | senepe sa ntlha se se sa fetolwang]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-1.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-2.jpg] 
 
 
Ka Motsheganong 2016 ke ne ka tsaya senepe sa lebitla la mo lefelong leo kwa Morocco. Mo khoutung ya ANSI ya senepe, ke ne ka fetola difapaano tsotlhe (†) go nna dinaledi (*). Ke ne ka boloka senepe se se fetotsweng jalo gape.

TITLE: Palogare ya mebala
SLUG: barevny-prumer
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barevny-prumer/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barevny-prumer.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barevny-prumer/index.html
============================================================
Palogare ya mebala
 
 Moranang 2017 
 
 
 
Mo karolong ya molema ya setshwantsho go na le 1156 ya dinepe mo rasetereng ya 34x34 px. Piksiele nngwe le nngwe ya mmala e emela mmala o o gare wa senepe se se rileng go tswa kwa nageng eo. Mo karolong ya moja ya setshwantsho ke mmala o o gare wa dipiksiele tsotlhe go tswa kwa karolong ya molema, ke gore, mmala o o gare wa dinepe tsotlhe go tswa kwa nageng eo.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png | Czech Republic]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png] 
Czech Republic
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-2.jpg | China]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-2.jpg] 
China
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-3.jpg | Ecuador]

============================================================
Dikhutwana tsa tswekere
 
 Tlhakole 2017 
 
 
 
Letoto la dinepe le tlhageletse ka nako ya diteko tsa talente kwa Sekolong se Segolo sa Kgwebo, Botaki jo bo Dirisiwang le Tlhabololo kwa Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Bakeng sa karolo ya diteko tsa go nka dinepe, re ne re letlelelwa go tlisa dilwana tsa rona, mme palo le bogolo jwa tsona di ne di lekanyeditswe. Ke tlisisitse lebokoso la dikotwana tsa sukiri, tse ke neng ke di rulaganya mo poleiting ka tsela ya rasetero e e rulaganyeditsweng sentle mme morago ka tshela metsi mo go tsona. Dikotwana tseo di ne tsa nna tsa nna tsa nyerologa, tsa wa, le go wa ka tsela ya mathata.

TITLE: Laeborari ya bonono ba mo mebileng
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Laeborari ya bonono ba mo mebileng
 
 Tlhakole 2017 
 
 
 
Go na le ditiro tse dintsi tse di kgatlhang tsa bonono mo mebileng go dikologa rona, go tloga ka disetikara tse dinnye, mabota a a takilweng, go ya go mefuta e e farologaneng ya 3D dilo. Ka ntlha ya gore bangwadi ba ditiro tseno ba tshwanetse go nna ba sa itsege ka ntlha ya go thulana le molao, lefatshe leno la bonono ba mo mebileng le itlhokomolosi ka botlalo mme le kgaogane le lefatshe la kgwebo. Ditiro tsa bonono ba mo mebileng tse re ka di bonang gongwe le gongwe mo toropong gantsi di nna di le ncha. Ditiro tse disha tsa bonono di khurumetsa tse di kgologolo, pente e a kgoboga mmogo le polasetara, dilo tsa 3D di nna tlhagala ya bosenyi, mme disetikara di a kgoboga kgotsa di a fela ka ntlha ya tikologo fa nako e ntse e ya. Ke tsamaiso ya tlholego, ntlha ya nako, le fa go le jalo ke dumela gore ditiro dingwe tsa bonono ba mo mebileng di tshwanelwa ke go okediwa botshelo.
 
Ke ka moo ke itiretseng tshwetso ya go kwala ditiro tse di kgatlhang thata. Ka boikaelelo joo, ka 2017 ke ne ka tlhoma tsebe ya Facebook e e bidiwang Street art knihovna (Laeborari ya bonono ba mo mebileng), kwa ke ntseng ke tsenya diposo gone go fitlha gompieno. Gore ke fitlhelele batho ba le bantsi, ke ne ka tlhoma tsebe ya leina le le tshwanang mo Instagram.
 
Mo ditsebeng tseno, ke leka go tsenya thata ditiro tse di tsayang mogopolo o o rileng, mme karolo ya botaki ya tiro ke e tsaya e le ya bobedi.
 
Laeborari ya bonono ba mo mebileng (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Laeborari ya bonono ba mo mebileng (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Setshwantsho sa tsebe ya Facebook]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Setshwantsho sa tsebe ya Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
Ga go re sepe gore o tswa kgaolong efe...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. Sedimonthole 2014 ke ne ke tsere senepe sa lobota lo lo kwadilweng mo Mombasa. Morago ga moo ke ne ka leka go ranola mokwalo o o mo go lone. Ke ne ka tsenya karolo nngwe ya lone e e balegang mo motšhineng wa go batla mo internet mme ka tsela eo ka bona senepe se se latelang.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

Bogodimo
 
 4. 2. 2016 - 3. 4. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 Galerie města Plzně: PELE, GOMPIENO, MORAGO 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/GALBEnzekBw?rel=0&showinfo=0]

TITLE: Go tloga kwa toropo go ya kwa go se se sa itlhaganelwang
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/
LANGUAGE: tn
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2016-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Go tloga kwa toropo go ya kwa go se se sa itlhaganelwang
 
 ferikgong 2016 
 
 
 
Ka ngwaga wa 2016 ke ne ka newa tiro ya go dira tsela ya go tshwantsha toropo ka mokgwa wa bonono, e le karolo ya go itokisetsa sekolo sa bonono kwa ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14. Ke ne ke na le kgatlhego ya go itse gore tsela eno ya go tshwantsha e ka ya kwa bokgakaleng bofe, ka jalo ke ne ka tswelela ke dira tiro eno go fitlha gompieno (ngwaga wa 2019). Go tloga mo tseleng e e motlhofo ya go tshwantsha toropo, ke ne ka fetela kwa go thubegeng ga ditsela tse di rulagantsweng sentle, tse di neng tsa baka kgatelelo e e kgatlhang go ya ka pono ya bonono. Go tswa foo, ke ne ka leka go dirisa se se sa itlhaganelwang mo tirong eno, mme ga go na sepe se ke se boneng se se ka bidiwang se se sa itlhaganelwang ruri se se se nang mabaka. Determinism e ne e nkgoreletsa ka metlha, ka jalo ke ne ke dirisa fela se se bidiwang pseudorandom (go na le lebaka le le ka supiwang, mme go thata go bolelela pele gore bokhutlo e tla nna eng, ka jalo go lebega e kete ke se se sa itlhaganelwang). Ditsela dingwe di ne tsa ntsenya mo mathateng, ka jalo ke ne ka tshwanelwa ke go batla pono e nngwe le tharabololo e nngwe ya go tshwantsha, jaaka go thulaganya tlhagiso ya pono, moiré effect, tatelano ya Fibonacci, jalo jalo. Letsholo la ditshwantsho tse di fa tlase ke bokhutlo jwa ditiro tse.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg | Mombasa: senepe sa mokwadi le ditshwantsho]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg]

============================================================
Mofero
 
 Motsheganong 2008 
 
 
 
Tiro ya kwa sekolong sa bana ba bannye.
 
Taelo e ne e le go sega dithunya tse di thunyang mo pampiring ya mebala le go di dira setshwantsho.
 
Go na le dithunya ke dirile plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
